===== SOURCE: tools/build_review_pack.py =====

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Build local review pack and copy/paste messages; no network calls."""
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
paths = [ROOT / "README.md", ROOT / "PROJECT.md", ROOT / "CONTEXT.md", ROOT / "AGENTS.md"]
for pattern in ["docs/*.md", "docs/adr/*.md", "research/*.md", "config/*.json", "contracts/*.md", "contracts/*.json", "examples/*.json", "tools/*.py", "tools/*.cjs", "design/*.json", "design/*.md", "prototype/*.html", "prototype/*.css", "prototype/*.js", "prototype/*.md"]:
    paths.extend(sorted(ROOT.glob(pattern)))
paths.extend([ROOT / "review/GPT-PRO-PROMPT.md", ROOT / "review/V02-CHANGELOG.md", ROOT / "review/V03-CHANGELOG.md", ROOT / "review/V03-COUNTEREXAMPLES.md", ROOT / "review/DESIGN-DETAIL-REVIEW.md"])
paths.extend(sorted(ROOT.glob("review/ROUND-2-*")))
if (ROOT / "review/validation-result.json").exists():
    paths.append(ROOT / "review/validation-result.json")
parts = ["# 价值投资策略管理系统 v0.3 · GPT Pro 整包评审材料\n\n日期：2026-10-01。产品范围已确认，工程设计尚未实现。只含本次新生成设计、合成例子及材料检查。\n\n请对照随附提示词完整审查；不能从材料检查通过推导运行时已通过。\n\n## 文件目录与 SHA-256\n\n| 文件 | SHA-256 |\n|---|---|\n"]
for p in paths:
    parts.append(f"| `{p.relative_to(ROOT)}` | `{hashlib.sha256(p.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}` |\n")
for p in paths:
    rel = p.relative_to(ROOT)
    parts.append(f"\n\n---\n\n# 文件：{rel}\n\n")
    text = p.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    if p.suffix == ".json":
        parts.append("```json\n" + text + "```\n")
    else:
        parts.append(text)
output = ROOT / "review/REVIEW-PACK.md"
output.write_text("".join(parts), encoding="utf-8")
print(f"Built {len(paths)} source sections; {output.stat().st_size} bytes; SHA256={hashlib.sha256(output.read_bytes()).hexdigest()}")

# Plain-text alternatives: no attachment and no repository access are required.
source_paths = [p for p in paths if str(p.relative_to(ROOT)) not in {"review/GPT-PRO-PROMPT.md", "review/validation-result.json"}]
sections = []
for p in source_paths:
    sections.append(f"\n\n===== SOURCE: {p.relative_to(ROOT)} =====\n\n" + p.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
instruction = (ROOT / "review/GPT-PRO-PROMPT.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8").split("## 完整提示词\n", 1)[1].strip()
all_text = instruction + "\n\n[REVIEW_MATERIALS_BEGIN]\n" + "".join(sections) + "\n[REVIEW_MATERIALS_END]\n请现在开始完整评审并输出 v0.3。\n"
(ROOT / "review/PASTE-ALL.txt").write_text(all_text, encoding="utf-8")
groups, group = [], ""
for section in sections:
    if group and len(group) + len(section) > 14000:
        groups.append(group)
        group = ""
    group += section
if group:
    groups.append(group)
links = []
for index, group in enumerate(groups, 1):
    start = instruction + "\n\n[REVIEW_MATERIALS_BEGIN]\n" if index == 1 else ""
    end = "\n[REVIEW_MATERIALS_END]\n全部材料已发完，请现在开始完整评审并输出 v0.3。\n" if index == len(groups) else "\n本段结束，材料尚未发完，请只确认已收到，不开始评审。\n"
    name = f"PASTE-PART-{index:02}.txt"
    body = start + f"\n[PART {index}/{len(groups)}]\n" + group + f"\n[END PART {index}/{len(groups)}]\n" + end
    (ROOT / "review" / name).write_text(body, encoding="utf-8")
    links.append(f"{index}. [{name}](./{name})：{len(body):,} 字符；直接复制全文作为一条消息。")
help_text = """# 不附文件的 GPT Pro 评审方式

## 优先：读取 GitHub

复制 [完整提示词](./GPT-PRO-PROMPT.md)中“完整提示词”下的全部内容，直接发给 GPT Pro。提示词已经指定仓库与材料路径，不要求上传附件。该方式要求当前 GPT Pro 会话有权限读取这个私有仓库；链接本身不赋予权限。

## 备用：正文粘贴

没有 GitHub 读取能力时，打开 [PASTE-ALL.txt](./PASTE-ALL.txt)，复制全部正文发给 GPT Pro，里面已有提示词和完整业务／工程／合同／合成示例，无需另发提示词或附件。无需 GitHub 权限也可在本地打开同名文本。

若单条消息长度受界面限制，按下列顺序复制各段到同一个对话。第1段已包含完整提示词；中间段仅接收，最后一段自动开始评审。每个原文件完整保留，不在文件中间截断。没有假定所有界面都支持一次粘贴整包。

""" + "\n".join(links) + "\n\n这些是当前v0.3的传递方式。纯文本按文件完整覆盖README、领域词汇、实现规则、全部设计/ADR/两轮研究/配置/Schema/合成例子与变更账本；不包含机器检查报告的冗长日志，该日志仍在仓库可查。\n"
(ROOT / "review/PASTE-INSTRUCTIONS.md").write_text(help_text, encoding="utf-8")
print(f"Built full copy/paste text: {len(all_text):,} characters; {len(groups)} file-boundary parts.")


===== SOURCE: tools/material_schema.py =====

"""Conservative checker for the Schema keywords used by these design fixtures.

Not a complete draft-2020-12 implementation. Unknown assertion keywords fail,
and external refs are forbidden. Product implementation must use a standard
validator plus business semantic validation.
"""
import re
import uuid
from datetime import datetime


class MaterialError(ValueError):
    pass


def validate(value, schema, root=None):
    root = root or schema
    allowed = {"$schema", "$defs", "$ref", "title", "description", "type", "enum",
               "const", "properties", "required", "additionalProperties", "items",
               "minLength", "maxLength", "minItems", "maxItems", "minProperties",
               "minimum", "maximum", "pattern", "format", "uniqueItems", "oneOf", "anyOf",
               "allOf", "not", "if", "then", "else"}
    if set(schema) - allowed:
        raise MaterialError(f"Unsupported keywords: {set(schema) - allowed}")
    if "$ref" in schema:
        ref = schema["$ref"]
        if not ref.startswith("#/"):
            raise MaterialError("External refs are not supported")
        target = root
        for part in ref[2:].split("/"):
            target = target[part.replace("~1", "/").replace("~0", "~")]
        validate(value, target, root)
    if "const" in schema and (value != schema["const"] or
                               isinstance(value, bool) != isinstance(schema["const"], bool)):
        raise MaterialError("const mismatch")
    if "enum" in schema and value not in schema["enum"]:
        raise MaterialError("enum mismatch")
    if "type" in schema:
        types = schema["type"] if isinstance(schema["type"], list) else [schema["type"]]
        predicates = {"object": lambda: isinstance(value, dict),
                      "array": lambda: isinstance(value, list),
                      "string": lambda: isinstance(value, str),
                      "boolean": lambda: isinstance(value, bool),
                      "null": lambda: value is None,
                      "integer": lambda: isinstance(value, int) and not isinstance(value, bool),
                      "number": lambda: isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool)}
        if not any(predicates[t]() for t in types):
            raise MaterialError("type mismatch")
    for keyword in ["oneOf", "anyOf", "allOf"]:
        if keyword in schema:
            matches = 0
            for option in schema[keyword]:
                try:
                    validate(value, option, root)
                    matches += 1
                except MaterialError:
                    pass
            wanted = matches == 1 if keyword == "oneOf" else (matches >= 1 if keyword == "anyOf" else matches == len(schema[keyword]))
            if not wanted:
                raise MaterialError(f"{keyword} mismatch")
    if "not" in schema:
        try:
            validate(value, schema["not"], root)
        except MaterialError:
            pass
        else:
            raise MaterialError("not mismatch")
    if "if" in schema:
        try:
            validate(value, schema["if"], root)
            branch = "then"
        except MaterialError:
            branch = "else"
        if branch in schema:
            validate(value, schema[branch], root)
    if isinstance(value, dict):
        if not set(schema.get("required", [])) <= value.keys():
            raise MaterialError("missing required property")
        if len(value) < schema.get("minProperties", 0):
            raise MaterialError("minProperties")
        props = schema.get("properties", {})
        for key, item in value.items():
            if key in props:
                validate(item, props[key], root)
            elif schema.get("additionalProperties") is False:
                raise MaterialError("extra property")
            elif isinstance(schema.get("additionalProperties"), dict):
                validate(item, schema["additionalProperties"], root)
    if isinstance(value, list):
        if not schema.get("minItems", 0) <= len(value) <= schema.get("maxItems", float("inf")):
            raise MaterialError("array length")
        if schema.get("uniqueItems"):
            if any(item in value[:i] for i, item in enumerate(value)):
                raise MaterialError("duplicate array item")
        for item in value:
            if "items" in schema:
                validate(item, schema["items"], root)
    if isinstance(value, str):
        if not schema.get("minLength", 0) <= len(value) <= schema.get("maxLength", float("inf")):
            raise MaterialError("string length")
        if "pattern" in schema and not re.search(schema["pattern"], value):
            raise MaterialError("pattern mismatch")
        if schema.get("format") == "uuid":
            try:
                if str(uuid.UUID(value)) != value.lower():
                    raise ValueError("not canonical")
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise MaterialError("uuid format") from exc
        elif schema.get("format") == "date-time":
            try:
                parsed = datetime.fromisoformat(value.replace("Z", "+00:00"))
                if "T" not in value or parsed.tzinfo is None:
                    raise ValueError("offset required")
            except ValueError as exc:
                raise MaterialError("date-time format") from exc
        elif "format" in schema:
            raise MaterialError("unsupported format")
    if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool):
        if not schema.get("minimum", -float("inf")) <= value <= schema.get("maximum", float("inf")):
            raise MaterialError("numeric bounds")


===== SOURCE: tools/render_database_design.py =====

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Render the physical-design dictionary from its local catalog. No database access."""
import json
from pathlib import Path
ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
catalog=json.loads((ROOT/'design/database-catalog.json').read_text())
tables={t['name']:t for t in catalog['tables']}
for t in tables.values():
    names=[c['name'] for c in t['columns']]
    assert len(names)==len(set(names)),t['name']
    for c in t['columns']:
        if 'references' in c:
            target,field=c['references'].split('.')
            assert target in tables and field in [x['name'] for x in tables[target]['columns']],c
    for key in t['unique_keys']:
        assert all(c in names for c in key),(t['name'],key)
header=(ROOT/'design/database-dictionary-intro.md').read_text()
parts=[header,'\n## 字段字典（由目录生成）\n\n']
for t in tables.values():
    parts.append(f"### {t['name']}\n\n责任：{t['owner']}；生命周期：`{t['lifecycle']}`；范围：`{t['scope']}`。\n\n")
    parts.append('| 字段 | PostgreSQL类型 | 可空 | 含义/引用 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n')
    for c in t['columns']:
        desc=c['meaning'].replace('|','/')
        if c.get('references'):desc+=f"；→ `{c['references']}`"
        parts.append(f"| `{c['name']}` | `{c['type']}` | {'是' if c['nullable'] else '否'} | {desc} |\n")
    parts.append('\n唯一键：'+ '；'.join('`('+','.join(k)+')`' for k in t['unique_keys'])+'。\n')
    if t['indexes']:parts.append('\n查询索引：'+'；'.join('`'+k+'`' for k in t['indexes'])+'。\n')
    if t['rules']:parts.append('\n'+ '\n'.join('- '+r for r in t['rules'])+'\n')
    parts.append('\n')
parts.append('## 逻辑对象与物理表示映射\n\n| 逻辑对象/视图 | 存储根 | 展开条件 | 说明 |\n|---|---|---|---|\n')
for v in catalog['logical_views']:parts.append(f"| `{v['name']}` | `{v['root']}` | `{v['filter']}` | {v['meaning']} |\n")
parts.append('\n这些视图尚未创建。统一修订存储只减少重复值和多态外键，公开Schema仍按现有类型分支；没有把risk/rubric能力收窄。\n')
(ROOT/'docs/15-database-dictionary.md').write_text(''.join(parts))
print(f"Rendered {len(tables)} tables, {sum(len(t['columns']) for t in tables.values())} columns; references and unique-key fields checked. No SQL executed.")


===== SOURCE: tools/v03_material_checks.py =====

"""Offline contract predicates and expected examples, never product acceptance."""
import copy
from decimal import Decimal
from datetime import datetime
from material_schema import validate


def run(read, check, reject, resolve, bundle, scoring, canonical_hash):
    # Shared typed payload copies cannot drift across the request and stored revision.
    command_schema=read('contracts/override-command.schema.json')
    human_schema=read('contracts/human-judgment.schema.json')
    check(command_schema['$defs']['judgment']==human_schema['$defs']['judgment'], 'v03 typed request/revision judgment agreement')
    decision_schema=read('contracts/review-decision.schema.json')
    replacements=read('examples/v03-human-replacements.json')['cases']
    for case in replacements:
        cmd=case['request'];response=case['expected_response'];rev=response['revision'];dec=response['decision']
        validate(cmd,command_schema);validate(rev,human_schema);validate(dec,decision_schema)
        check(rev['judgment']==cmd['replacement'] and rev['slot_id']==cmd['slot_id']==dec['subject_slot'], 'v03 replacement value / slot: '+case['id'])
        check(dec['subject_revision']==rev['revision_id'] and rev['previous_revision_id']!=rev['revision_id'] and dec['status']=='accepted' and dec['actor_kind']=='HUMAN', 'v03 immutable revision/decision references: '+case['id'])
        match=case['headers']['If-Match'].rsplit('-', 1)[-1]
        check(dec['supersedes_decision_id'] and response['slot_generation']==int(match)+1, 'v03 single If-Match CAS expectation: '+case['id'])
        check(set(cmd['replacement']['value']['evidence_ids'])<=set(cmd['evidence_ids']), 'v03 replacement evidence bound: '+case['id'])
        bad=copy.deepcopy(cmd);del bad['replacement'];reject(lambda:validate(bad,command_schema),'v03 replace without full value')
        bad=copy.deepcopy(cmd);bad['subject_revision_id']=rev['previous_revision_id'];reject(lambda:validate(bad,command_schema),'v03 replace mixed with subject reference')
    for action in ['accept','reject','pending','release']:
        cmd=copy.deepcopy(replacements[0]['request']);del cmd['replacement'];cmd.update(action=action,subject_revision_id=replacements[0]['expected_response']['revision']['previous_revision_id'])
        validate(cmd,command_schema);check(True,'v03 non-replacement action shape: '+action)
        bad=copy.deepcopy(cmd);bad['replacement']=replacements[0]['request']['replacement'];reject(lambda:validate(bad,command_schema),'v03 non-replace carries replacement: '+action)
    # Material target-resolution counterexamples. These predicates are not runtime DecisionService.
    fixture=read('examples/v03-risk-targets.json');policy=read('config/risk-target-policy-v1.json')
    risk_schema=read('contracts/analysis-result.schema.json')['properties']['risk_proposals']['items']
    def targets(p):
        validate(p,risk_schema)
        if p['company_id']!=fixture['identity_fixture']['company_id']:raise ValueError('company outside candidates')
        if p['target_kind'] not in policy['allowed_target_kinds'][p['risk_code']]:raise ValueError('risk kind')
        securities=fixture['identity_fixture']['securities']
        if p['target_kind']=='company':
            if p['target_id']!=p['company_id']:raise ValueError('company target')
            return [s['id'] for s in securities]
        matches=[s['id'] for s in securities if s['id' if p['target_kind']=='security' else 'primary_listing_id']==p['target_id']]
        if not matches:raise ValueError('target outside candidates')
        return matches
    a,b=fixture['proposals']
    check(targets(a)==fixture['expected']['listing_risk_targets'],'v03 H listing-only expected resolution')
    check(targets(b)==fixture['expected']['company_risk_targets'],'v03 company default expected resolution A/H')
    check(set(targets(b))==set(targets(a))|set(targets(b)), 'v03 removal of listing risk leaves independent company risk set')
    for changes,label in [({'target_kind':'company','target_id':a['company_id']},'delisting cannot broaden to company'),({'target_id':'00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000999999'},'target outside manifest'),({'company_id':'00000000-0000-4000-8000-000000999999'},'wrong parent company')]:
        bad=copy.deepcopy(a);bad.update(changes);reject(lambda:targets(bad),'v03 '+label)
    old=copy.deepcopy(a);del old['target_kind'];del old['target_id'];reject(lambda:validate(old,risk_schema),'v03 ambiguous company-only old risk rejected')
    # Registry / inheritance / custom contribution whitelist shape and material semantics.
    registry=read('config/dimensions-standard-v1.json');validate(registry,read('contracts/dimension-registry.schema.json'));check(True,'v03 registry shape')
    custom=read('examples/v03-template-custom.json');templates=copy.deepcopy(bundle['templates'])+[custom]
    resolved=resolve(custom['template_key'],templates)
    check(sum(Decimal(v) for v in resolved['dimension_weights'].values())==1 and resolved['dimension_policies']['x_customer_retention']['event_policy']=={'enabled':True,'half_life_days':90},'v03 custom dimension resolved weight/event golden')
    q=resolved['dimension_policies']['governance']['quality_policy']
    check(q['freshness']['max_age_days']==90 and q['evidence_requirement']=='issuer_or_regulator_original','v03 complete policy patch golden')
    check(scoring['dimension_policies']['governance']['quality_policy']['freshness']['max_age_days']==180,'v03 base 180-day policy unchanged')
    check(120<180 and not 120<90 and not 90<90,'v03 120-day freshness / exact expiry boundary arithmetic, NOT runtime freshness')
    check(min(365,180)==180,'v03 restrictive strategy age composition arithmetic')
    for changes,label in [({'dimension':'x_not_registered'},'unknown custom dimension'),({'event_policy':{'enabled':True,'half_life_days':0}},'invalid event half-life'),({'disabled':True},'disabled mixed with patch')]:
        ts=copy.deepcopy(templates);ts[-1]['patches'][-1].update(changes);reject(lambda:resolve(custom['template_key'],ts),'v03 '+label)
    # Blueprint policy references are frozen: a different content hash cannot load silently.
    bad=copy.deepcopy(templates);bad[0]['base_config_ref']['sha256']='0'*64;reject(lambda:resolve(custom['template_key'],bad),'v03 old parent hash not hot-replaced')
    impact=copy.deepcopy(read('examples/analysis-proposal.json')['impact_proposals'][0]);impact.update(dimension='x_customer_retention',half_life_days=90)
    impact_schema=read('contracts/analysis-result.schema.json')['properties']['impact_proposals']['items']
    def allow(p,c):
        validate(p,impact_schema)
        if p['dimension'] not in c['dimension_weights']:raise ValueError('dimension not active')
        event=c['dimension_policies'][p['dimension']]['event_policy']
        if not event['enabled'] or p['half_life_days']!=event['half_life_days']:raise ValueError('event policy')
    allow(impact,resolved);check(True,'v03 registered active custom impact material allowlist')
    bad=copy.deepcopy(impact);bad['dimension']='x_not_registered';reject(lambda:allow(bad,resolved),'v03 unregistered impact')
    bad=copy.deepcopy(impact);bad['half_life_days']=30;reject(lambda:allow(bad,resolved),'v03 model cannot alter event half-life')
    ts=copy.deepcopy(templates);ts[-1]['patches'][-1]['event_policy']={'enabled':False};baseline_only=resolve(custom['template_key'],ts)
    check(baseline_only['baselines']['x_customer_retention']==resolved['baselines']['x_customer_retention'],'v03 baseline-only retains baseline')
    check('x_customer_retention' not in baseline_only['event_contribution']['half_life_calendar_days'],'v03 baseline-only no fictitious half-life')
    reject(lambda:allow(impact,baseline_only),'v03 baseline-only refuses event proposal')
    check(resolve('company-synthetic-manufacturer')['baselines']==scoring['baselines'],'v03 custom template does not mutate old standard chain')
    # Only temporal boundary / command shape assertions. No seal/token or SQL execution exists.
    seal=read('examples/v03-seal-command.json');validate(seal,read('contracts/evaluation-seal.schema.json'));check(True,'v03 seal command shape')
    manifest=read('examples/v03-seal-manifest.json');manifest_schema=read('contracts/evaluation-input-manifest.schema.json')
    validate(manifest,manifest_schema)
    check(canonical_hash(manifest)==seal['manifest_sha256'] and manifest['manifest_id']==seal['manifest_id'], 'v03 frozen manifest hash/reference binding')
    check(all(manifest[k]==seal[k] for k in ['release_id','security_id','primary_listing_id','market_session','evaluation_as_of','knowledge_cutoff']), 'v03 seal/manifest fixed context agreement')
    bad=copy.deepcopy(manifest);del bad['inputs']['human_overrides'];reject(lambda:validate(bad,manifest_schema),'v03 manifest cannot omit input class')
    bad=copy.deepcopy(manifest);bad['inputs']['price']['inputs']=[];reject(lambda:validate(bad,manifest_schema),'v03 valid price class without input')
    bad=copy.deepcopy(manifest);bad['inputs']['price']={'quality':'missing','inputs':[],'reason_codes':[]};reject(lambda:validate(bad,manifest_schema),'v03 missing input without quality reason')
    check(all(datetime.fromisoformat(i['known_at'])<=datetime.fromisoformat(manifest['knowledge_cutoff']) for g in manifest['inputs'].values() for i in g['inputs']), 'v03 manifest fixture does not include late known input')
    cutoff=datetime.fromisoformat(seal['knowledge_cutoff']);asof=datetime.fromisoformat(seal['evaluation_as_of'])
    check((cutoff-asof).total_seconds()==3600,'v03 seal fixture fixed 60 minute cutoff')
    check(15<60 and 40<=60 and 50<=60 and 61>60 and 65>=60,'v03 +15/+40/+50/+60/+61/+65 boundary arithmetic; token/concurrency NOT RUN')
    bad=copy.deepcopy(seal);bad['worker_now']='2026-09-30T09:20:00Z';reject(lambda:validate(bad,read('contracts/evaluation-seal.schema.json')),'v03 seal cannot replace cutoff with caller now')


===== SOURCE: tools/validate_design.py =====

#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Offline v0.3 material checks. This does NOT execute the proposed application."""
import copy
import hashlib
import json
import re
import subprocess
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from decimal import Decimal, localcontext, ROUND_HALF_EVEN
from pathlib import Path
from urllib.parse import unquote
from material_schema import validate, MaterialError

ROOT=Path(__file__).resolve().parents[1]
checks=[]
def check(ok,name):
    if not ok:raise AssertionError(name)
    checks.append(name)
def read(path):return json.loads((ROOT/path).read_text())
def sha(path):return hashlib.sha256(path.read_bytes()).hexdigest()
def canonical_hash(value):return hashlib.sha256((json.dumps(value,ensure_ascii=False,indent=2)+'\n').encode()).hexdigest()
def current_revision():
    try:
        return subprocess.run(['git','rev-parse','HEAD'],cwd=ROOT,check=True,capture_output=True,text=True).stdout.strip()
    except (OSError,subprocess.CalledProcessError):
        return None
def reject(fn,name):
    try:fn()
    except (MaterialError,ValueError,AssertionError,KeyError):checks.append('Negative: '+name)
    else:raise AssertionError('Expected rejection: '+name)

for pattern in ['config/*.json','contracts/*.json','examples/*.json','design/*.json']:
    for p in sorted(ROOT.glob(pattern)):
        json.loads(p.read_text());check(True,f'JSON syntax: {p.relative_to(ROOT)}')
for p in sorted(ROOT.rglob('*.md')):
    if p.name=='REVIEW-PACK.md' or '.git' in p.parts or 'local-evidence' in p.parts:continue
    for dest in re.findall(r'(?<!!)\[[^\]]+\]\(([^\s)]+)\)',p.read_text()):
        if '://' in dest or dest.startswith('#'):continue
        target=unquote(dest.split('#')[0]);check((p.parent/target).exists(),f'Local link: {p.relative_to(ROOT)} -> {target}')
for p in sorted(ROOT.glob('contracts/*.schema.json')):
    s=json.loads(p.read_text());check(s.get('$schema')=='https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema',f'Schema dialect: {p.name}')
    def inspect(v):
        if isinstance(v,dict):
            if '$ref' in v:
                ref=v['$ref'];check(ref.startswith('#/'),f'Local-only schema ref: {p.name}')
                t=s
                for seg in ref[2:].split('/'):t=t[seg.replace('~1','/').replace('~0','~')]
                check(isinstance(t,dict),f'Resolvable schema ref: {p.name}:{ref}')
            for child in v.values():inspect(child)
        elif isinstance(v,list):
            for child in v:inspect(child)
    inspect(s)

pairs=[('config/strategy-standard-v1.json','strategy.schema.json'),('config/scoring-standard-v1.json','scoring.schema.json'),('examples/analysis-proposal.json','analysis-result.schema.json'),('examples/job-event.json','job-event.schema.json'),('examples/auto-decision.json','review-decision.schema.json'),('examples/human-override.json','review-decision.schema.json')]
for filename,schemafile in pairs:
    value,s=read(filename),read('contracts/'+schemafile);validate(value,s);check(True,f'Limited Schema fixture validation: {filename}')
    bad=copy.deepcopy(value);bad['unexpected_material_field']=True
    reject(lambda:validate(bad,s),filename+' extra field')
    bad=copy.deepcopy(value);del bad[s['required'][0]]
    reject(lambda:validate(bad,s),filename+' missing required field')

strategy=read('config/strategy-standard-v1.json');scoring=read('config/scoring-standard-v1.json');metrics=read('config/metric-definitions-v2.json')['metrics'];rubrics=read('config/rubrics-standard-v1.json')['rubrics']
all_rubrics={**rubrics,**read('config/rubrics-synthetic-v03.json')['rubrics']}
registry=read('config/dimensions-standard-v1.json');registry_entries={x['dimension']:x for x in registry['entries']}
check(len(registry_entries)==len(registry['entries']),'Unique registered dimension IDs')
check(scoring['dimension_registry_ref']['sha256']==sha(ROOT/scoring['dimension_registry_ref']['path']),'Pinned dimension registry content hash')
check(strategy['universe']['markets']==['CN_A','HK'],'Confirmed A/H scope')
check(strategy['scoring_model_ref']==scoring['model_key'],'Strategy scoring reference')
check(scoring['metric_definition_ref']=='metric-definitions-v2','Corrected metric-definition version')
check(scoring['numeric_policy_ref']==read('config/numeric-policy-v1.json')['policy_key'],'Numeric policy reference')
check(sum(Decimal(v) for v in scoring['dimension_weights'].values())==1,'Base dimension weights sum to 1')
check(strategy['quality_gates']['minimum_coverage']==scoring['minimum_company_coverage']=='0.80','Coverage consistency')
check('financial_max_announced_age_days' not in strategy['quality_gates'],'Removed uniform announcement age')
check(strategy['quality_gates']['price_max_lag_sessions']==0,'No stale close advances FINAL confirmation')
for dim,b in scoring['baselines'].items():
    if 'metrics' in b:
        check(sum(Decimal(x['weight']) for x in b['metrics'])==1,f'Submetric weights: {dim}')
        check(all(x['key'] in metrics and Decimal(x['zero_at'])!=Decimal(x['full_at']) for x in b['metrics']),f'Metric registry / ranges: {dim}')
    else:check(b['rubric_ref'] in rubrics and b['method']=='accepted_evidence_rubric',f'AUTO/HUMAN rubric ref: {dim}')
check(sum(len(v['criteria']) for v in rubrics.values())==11,'Eleven rubric criteria supplied')
for name,r in rubrics.items():
    check(all(len(c['anchors'])==5 and all(c['anchors']) for c in r['criteria']),f'Five evidence anchors: {name}')
    check(r['minimum_criterion_coverage']=='0.80',f'Rubric coverage: {name}')

fields=set(read('contracts/strategy.schema.json')['$defs']['expression']['oneOf'][1]['properties']['field']['enum'])
for mode in ['enter','retain']:
    clauses=strategy[mode]['all'];check(0<len(clauses)<=30 and len({x['field'] for x in clauses})==len(clauses),f'Bounded unique default clauses: {mode}')
    for clause in clauses:
        check(clause['field'] in fields and clause['op'] in {'gte','lte'},f'Whitelisted rule: {mode}:{clause["field"]}');Decimal(clause['value'])
a={x['field']:x for x in strategy['enter']['all']};b={x['field']:x for x in strategy['retain']['all']};check(set(a)==set(b),'Enter/retain field parity')
for f,e in a.items():
    v,w=Decimal(e['value']),Decimal(b[f]['value']);check(e['op']==b[f]['op'] and (v>=w if e['op']=='gte' else v<=w),f'Retain no stricter than enter: {f}')
p=strategy['state_policy'];check(p['enter_distinct_sessions']==p['exit_distinct_sessions']==2 and p['require_adjacent_expected_sessions'] and p['final_price_required'],'Two adjacent expected FINAL sessions')
check(p['notification_phase']=='deferred' and not p['initial_baseline_notify'] and not p['historical_notify'],'Notifications deferred, baseline/history quiet')

# Material resolver: demonstrates the specified inheritance semantics; it is not application implementation.
bundle=read('config/templates-standard-v1.json');tplschema=read('contracts/scoring-template.schema.json')
for t in bundle['templates']:validate(t,tplschema);check(True,'Limited template shape: '+t['template_key'])
def resolve(key,templates=None,stack=()):
    templates=templates or bundle['templates'];bykey={t['template_key']:t for t in templates}
    if len(bykey)!=len(templates):raise ValueError('duplicate template key')
    t=bykey[key]
    validate(t,tplschema)
    if t['scope']['kind'] != {'base':'all','industry':'industry','company':'company'}[t['level']]:raise ValueError('scope/level mismatch')
    if key in stack:raise ValueError('cycle')
    if t['level']=='base':
        if t['base_config_ref']['sha256']!=sha(ROOT/t['base_config_ref']['path']):raise ValueError('base hash')
        c=copy.deepcopy(read(t['base_config_ref']['path']))
        if c['dimension_registry_ref']['sha256']!=sha(ROOT/c['dimension_registry_ref']['path']):raise ValueError('registry hash')
    else:
        parent=bykey[t['parent_ref']['key']]
        if t['parent_ref']['version']!=parent['version'] or t['parent_ref']['sha256']!=canonical_hash(parent):raise ValueError('parent version/hash')
        if (parent['level'],t['level']) not in {('base','industry'),('industry','company')}:raise ValueError('level')
        c=resolve(parent['template_key'],templates,stack+(key,))
    seen=set()
    for patch in t['patches']:
        d=patch['dimension']
        if d in seen:raise ValueError('duplicate patch')
        seen.add(d)
        if d not in registry_entries:raise ValueError('unregistered dimension')
        if t['level']=='industry' and t['scope']['industry_key'] not in registry_entries[d]['applicable_industries']:raise ValueError('inapplicable dimension')
        if t['level']=='company':
            industry=bykey[t['parent_ref']['key']]['scope']['industry_key']
            if industry not in registry_entries[d]['applicable_industries']:raise ValueError('inapplicable dimension')
        if patch.get('disabled'):
            if set(patch)-{'dimension','reason','disabled'}:raise ValueError('mixed disable')
            c['dimension_weights'].pop(d,None);c['baselines'].pop(d,None);c['dimension_policies'].pop(d,None)
        else:
            if d not in c['dimension_weights'] and not(d.startswith('x_') and 'weight' in patch and 'baseline' in patch):raise ValueError('unregistered new dimension')
            if d not in c['dimension_policies']:c['dimension_policies'][d]={k:copy.deepcopy(registry_entries[d][k]) for k in ['quality_policy','event_policy']}
            if 'weight' in patch:c['dimension_weights'][d]=patch['weight']
            if 'baseline' in patch:c['baselines'][d]=copy.deepcopy(patch['baseline'])
            for key in ['quality_policy','event_policy']:
                if key in patch:c['dimension_policies'][d][key]=copy.deepcopy(patch[key])
    if sum(Decimal(x) for x in c['dimension_weights'].values())!=1:raise ValueError('weight sum')
    if set(c['baselines'])!=set(c['dimension_weights']):raise ValueError('dimensions/weights')
    if not set(strategy['quality_gates']['required_dimensions'])<=set(c['baselines']):raise ValueError('required disabled')
    if set(c['dimension_policies'])!=set(c['baselines']):raise ValueError('policies/dimensions')
    c['event_contribution']['half_life_calendar_days']={d:v['event_policy']['half_life_days'] for d,v in c['dimension_policies'].items() if v['event_policy']['enabled']}
    validate(c,read('contracts/scoring.schema.json'))
    for d,base in c['baselines'].items():
        if base['method'] not in registry_entries[d]['allowed_baseline_methods']:raise ValueError('baseline method')
        freshness=c['dimension_policies'][d]['quality_policy']['freshness']['kind']
        if (base['method']=='weighted_metric_rubric')!=(freshness=='report_obligation'):raise ValueError('freshness method')
        validate(base,read('contracts/scoring.schema.json')['properties']['baselines']['additionalProperties'])
        if 'metrics' in base and (sum(Decimal(x['weight']) for x in base['metrics'])!=1 or any(Decimal(x['zero_at'])==Decimal(x['full_at']) for x in base['metrics'])):raise ValueError('submetric weights/range')
        if 'metrics' in base and not all(x['key'] in metrics for x in base['metrics']):raise ValueError('unknown metric')
        if 'rubric_ref' in base and base['rubric_ref'] not in all_rubrics:raise ValueError('unknown rubric')
    return c
industry=resolve('industry-manufacturing');company=resolve('company-synthetic-manufacturer')
check(industry['dimension_weights']['profit_quality']=='0.30','Industry override golden value')
check(company['dimension_weights']=={'profit_quality':'0.35','financial_resilience':'0.20','business_model':'0.25','governance':'0.15','growth_sustainability':'0.05'},'Company override full golden weights')
check(company['baselines']==scoring['baselines'],'Unchanged baselines inherited')
for label,mutator in [
('bad parent hash',lambda ts:ts[-1]['parent_ref'].update(sha256='0'*64)),
('weight sum invalid',lambda ts:ts[-1]['patches'][0].update(weight='0.99')),
('duplicate patch',lambda ts:ts[-1]['patches'].append(copy.deepcopy(ts[-1]['patches'][0]))),
('disable required dimension',lambda ts:ts[-1]['patches'].append({'dimension':'business_model','disabled':True,'reason':'negative'})),
('unknown metric',lambda ts:ts[-1]['patches'][0].update(baseline={'method':'weighted_metric_rubric','metrics':[{'key':'unknown','weight':'1','zero_at':'0','full_at':'1'}]})),
('wrong scope',lambda ts:ts[-1].update(scope={'kind':'all'})),
('jump level',lambda ts:ts[-1]['parent_ref'].update(key=ts[0]['template_key'],version=1,sha256=canonical_hash(ts[0]))),
]:
    bad=copy.deepcopy(bundle['templates']);mutator(bad);reject(lambda:resolve('company-synthetic-manufacturer',bad),label)

policy=read('config/auto-review-policy-v1.json');roles=read('config/roles-standard-v1.json')['roles']
check(policy['risk_target_policy_ref']['sha256']==sha(ROOT/policy['risk_target_policy_ref']['path']),'Pinned risk target policy hash')
check(policy['hard_risk']['allow_auto_accept'] and not policy['template_and_strategy_auto_publish'],'AUTO risk allowed, templates/strategy human-published')
check(policy['human_override_precedence']=='until_released_or_expired' and policy['on_override_expiry']=='reevaluate_current_legal_inputs','Human override precedence/expiry')
check(set(roles)=={'viewer','researcher','strategy_manager','data_admin','system_admin'},'Five confirmed roles')
check('analysis.override' in roles['researcher'] and 'strategy.publish' not in roles['researcher'],'Researcher override but no publish')
check('template.publish' in roles['strategy_manager'] and 'source.manage' not in roles['strategy_manager'],'Strategy manager boundaries')
check('role.assign' not in roles['data_admin'] and 'analysis.override' not in roles['viewer'],'Data admin / viewer boundaries')

with localcontext() as ctx:
    ctx.prec=70;ctx.rounding=ROUND_HALF_EVEN;q=Decimal('0.000000000001')
    v=Decimal(10)*Decimal('.8')*Decimal('.5')*Decimal('.95')*Decimal('.8')

    check((v/Decimal(2).sqrt()).quantize(q)==Decimal('2.149604614807'),'Non-integer half-life golden arithmetic (sqrt cross-check)')
    check(Decimal(75)/Decimal(100)==Decimal('.75') and Decimal('.75')<Decimal('.8'),'Matched consolidated CFO/profit counterexample')
    check(Decimal('69.999999999999')<Decimal(70) and round(Decimal('69.999999999999'),1)==Decimal('70.0'),'Displayed 70.0 does not pass exact 70 threshold')

===== SOURCE: design/database-dictionary-intro.md =====

# 15 数据库字段设计与 ER 图

2026-10-01 · v0.3 物理设计候选；无DDL、迁移或数据库操作。本文件由[数据库目录](../design/database-catalog.json)与[说明源文件](../design/database-dictionary-intro.md)生成，字段以目录为唯一维护入口；运行 `python3 tools/render_database_design.py` 更新。04拥有逻辑分层/时间规范，11、12拥有业务语义，本文负责物理字段与存储映射。

## 1. 设计取舍与实施顺序

完整字段目录覆盖既有逻辑对象及页面操作所需持久化对象；它不是M0必须一次建完的建表清单。按首个纵向路径建立需要的表，其余随对应业务加入。

| 步骤 | 先落实的对象 | 可演示业务 | 关联需求/验收 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 最小基础 | workspace、user/membership、job/audit/outbox、manifest | 合成身份与可追溯动作 | R-11/12/17，T-14/20/29 |
| 资料与时间线 | source/policy、item/revision/observation、evidence、company/security/listing、event/fact | 合成材料到公司研究 | R-01…04，T-01…07/33 |
| 判断与覆盖 | analysis、slot、judgment/human revision、decision、idempotency | 自动判断及单项人工替换 | R-03/16，T-27/28/39 |
| 评分与估值 | financial/price/FX/义务、template/config/binding、baseline/contribution/snapshot | 三层模板解释及A/H分估值 | R-05/06/15，T-08…10/25/26/41/42 |
| 策略与变化 | draft/version/preview/release/binding、seal/evaluation/membership/transition/risk | 模拟发布及ENTER/EXIT/RISK | R-07/08，T-13/31/40/43 |
| 协作与恢复 | watchlist/note/view、correction、inbox/checkpoint/journal receipt | 自选、笔记、失败项恢复、更正 | R-09/10/12，T-18/19/32/34 |

采用统一 `judgment_revision` 存储完整类型化值，`item_company_link/event_company_impact/rubric_revision/risk_revision` 展开为逻辑视图，避免四份重复的有效指针及多态悬空外键。人工链表单独记录previous_revision并可指向AUTO原修订；JSONB按既有Schema和服务端语义校验，不代表可以自由塞任意字段。常用身份和有效指针有结构化键；后续只有实际查询需要才增加表达式索引。

已有04将部分对象合写一行，这里按生命周期拆开草稿、发布、修订和关联。自选、模拟预览、日历、幂等响应和当前评分指针是已有API/页面能力的存储补齐，未新增业务范围。通知表、向量表、生产分区、RLS与身份服务内部表后置。

## 2. 共用字段、外键和约束责任

- 所有表有服务端UUID `id` 和DB实际 `created_at`。组织内表有 `workspace_id` 与唯一 `(workspace_id,id)`；外键引用同组织表时必须用 `(workspace_id,ref_id)` 复合外键，不能只按id关联。`workspace`、`app_user`为全局身份，user引用由成员关系再验证范围。
- 首slice将市场身份/事实也放在各合成workspace，避免隐式共享。后续只有合法公共事实可迁入独立只读共享层；私有判断、笔记和策略不共享。
- `!`在目录作者格式代表必填，生成字典统一显示可空；null不等于0或无限有效。金额/比率`numeric(38,12)`、分数`numeric(15,12)`、权重`numeric(13,12)`是持久化候选，不改变12的Decimal算法/精度。超范围/不匹配单位明确invalid，不静默截断。
- 外键本身不能保证两个孩子指向同一父对象：例如decision的revision属于slot、primary listing属于security、score属于被评估company。提交服务负责关系校验；稳定且频繁的归属可采用复合父键。跨workspace约束在数据库层强制。
- 必须的DB约束：PK/FK/业务唯一键、非负版本/序号、范围值（权重/置信度0–1、signed_impact −1–1、分数0–100）、有效区间 `end IS NULL OR end>start`、cutoff≤generated_at、状态枚举。API/服务负责模板父链/权重/引用适用性、JSON Schema、证据权利和角色。
- JSON对象内容hash使用固定规范化算法，不依赖PostgreSQL JSONB文本输出。引用hash与存储payload一致。业务运行输入通过manifest固定内容版本；path/最新key不够。
- 当前指针和草稿允许更新，修订、发布、已封存评估不允许覆写。实现阶段限制对应服务写入口，不为了本轮设计增加全量触发器平台。
- 循环外键（source↔policy、security↔listing、slot↔decision、seal↔evaluation）在迁移中先建表后加FK；通过可延迟约束/同事务完成必要初始化。提交后不能留无效指针。
- nullable业务唯一键必须使用适用PG版本的 `NULLS NOT DISTINCT` 或明确的非空规范键；不得误以为普通UNIQUE能禁止多个NULL。

知识序列只是辅助顺序，不能单凭先分配sequence就认定已提交。实现要把输入发布可见性、真实known_at与cutoff watermark共同验证：跨cutoff的未提交事务不能回填已封存manifest。T-43须覆盖迟到提交；本轮未声称已实现提交时钟协议。

## 3. ER 图：资料与身份

```mermaid
erDiagram
    WORKSPACE ||--o{ SOURCE : owns
    SOURCE ||--|{ SOURCE_POLICY : versioned
    SOURCE ||--o{ INGESTION_RUN : captures
    SOURCE ||--o{ INFORMATION_ITEM : publishes
    INFORMATION_ITEM ||--|{ ITEM_REVISION : content
    INFORMATION_ITEM ||--|{ ITEM_OBSERVATION : observations
    ITEM_REVISION ||--o{ ITEM_OBSERVATION : observed_content
    RAW_OBJECT ||--o{ ITEM_REVISION : original
    ITEM_REVISION ||--o{ EVIDENCE : locates
    COMPANY ||--o{ SECURITY : issues
    SECURITY ||--|{ LISTING : quotations
    COMPANY ||--o{ COMPANY_ALIAS : names
    EVENT ||--|{ EVENT_REVISION : revisions
    EVENT_REVISION ||--o{ EVENT_EVIDENCE : claims
    EVIDENCE ||--o{ EVENT_EVIDENCE : supports
    EVENT_REVISION ||--o{ EVENT_FACT : contains
    ECONOMIC_FACT ||--o{ EVENT_FACT : same_fact
```

## 4. ER 图：判断、配置与评分

```mermaid
erDiagram
    COMPANY ||--o{ DECISION_SLOT : questions
    DECISION_SLOT ||--o{ JUDGMENT_REVISION : values
    JUDGMENT_REVISION ||--o| HUMAN_JUDGMENT_REVISION : human_lineage
    JUDGMENT_REVISION ||--o{ REVIEW_DECISION : decisions
    DECISION_SLOT ||--o{ REVIEW_DECISION : accepted_pointer
    SCORING_TEMPLATE ||--o{ TEMPLATE_DRAFT : edits
    SCORING_TEMPLATE ||--o{ TEMPLATE_VERSION : publishes
    TEMPLATE_VERSION o|--o{ TEMPLATE_VERSION : pinned_parent
    TEMPLATE_VERSION ||--o{ EFFECTIVE_CONFIG : resolves
    EFFECTIVE_CONFIG ||--o{ SCORING_BINDING : binds
    COMPANY ||--o{ BASELINE_DIMENSION : baseline
    ECONOMIC_FACT ||--o{ CONTRIBUTION_SLOT : deduplicates
    BASELINE_DIMENSION ||--o{ BASELINE_ABSORPTION : absorbs
    CONTRIBUTION_SLOT ||--o{ BASELINE_ABSORPTION : absorbed_version
    COMPANY ||--o{ SCORE_SNAPSHOT : quality
    SCORE_SNAPSHOT ||--o{ SCORE_DIMENSION : explains
    SCORE_DIMENSION ||--o{ SCORE_CONTRIBUTION : contributions
    SECURITY ||--o{ METRIC_SNAPSHOT : valuation
```

## 5. ER 图：策略与变化

```mermaid
erDiagram
    STRATEGY ||--o{ STRATEGY_DRAFT : edits
    STRATEGY ||--o{ STRATEGY_VERSION : config_versions
    STRATEGY_VERSION ||--o{ STRATEGY_RELEASE : releases
    PREVIEW ||--o| STRATEGY_RELEASE : compared
    STRATEGY_RELEASE ||--o{ RELEASE_COMPANY_BINDING : pins_config
    SCORING_BINDING ||--o{ RELEASE_COMPANY_BINDING : config
    STRATEGY_RELEASE ||--o{ RELEASE_SECURITY : pins_listing
    STRATEGY_RELEASE ||--o{ EVALUATION_SEAL : sessions
    EVALUATION_SEAL ||--o{ SEAL_CANDIDATE : attempts
    INPUT_MANIFEST ||--o{ SEAL_CANDIDATE : frozen_input
    STRATEGY_RELEASE ||--o{ STRATEGY_EVALUATION : results
    STRATEGY_RELEASE ||--o{ STRATEGY_MEMBERSHIP : states
    SECURITY ||--o{ STRATEGY_MEMBERSHIP : independent
    STRATEGY_MEMBERSHIP ||--o{ MEMBERSHIP_TRANSITION : history
    STRATEGY_EVALUATION o|--o{ MEMBERSHIP_TRANSITION : ordinary_basis
    JUDGMENT_REVISION ||--o{ RISK_RESOLUTION : risk_scope
    CORRECTION_RUN ||--o{ CORRECTION_RECORD : historical_notes
```

图强调业务关联，完整字段/FK以目录为准；不是要求每个阅读路径都做多表实时联查，列表可使用由事实库快照形成的读模型。

## 6. 写入事务与页面读取

| 动作 | 同事务必需内容 | 读取/页面结果 | 冲突/失败 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 人工replace | judgment_revision+human lineage+decision+slot CAS+idempotency+audit/outbox | 新值、作用对象、重算queued | 全回滚；409保留用户草稿 |
| 解除覆盖 | audit解除记录+清人工有效指针/提升generation+固定重评job/outbox | 人工覆盖已解除，当前等待合法新判断 | 不恢复过期旧AUTO |
| 基准吸收 | 新baseline+absorption+contribution指针+outbox | 新基准与扣除贡献同时可见 | 不出现双计中间结果 |
| 发布 | 验证preview/draft版本+固定config/binding/release+active指针+audit/outbox | CONFIG_CHANGE新基线 | 过期预览不创建半个release |
| 最终封存/应用 | evaluation+seal token+membership CAS+transition解释+audit/outbox | 唯一最终session和状态 | 旧fence拒绝；新风险优先 |
| 即时风险 | 有效risk decision/slot+适用resolution+membership风险代次+RISK解释+audit/outbox | 仅适用证券风险变化 | 不要求等待收盘evaluation |
| 历史更正 | 原记录保留+correction结果+当前必要reconciliation CAS+audit/outbox | 当时记录/更正说明可并列 | 旧generation不覆盖今天 |

当前评分查询取 `score_current` 指针但校验其状态、当前判断/权限代次；历史页直接取固定snapshot。候选按release/security分页，时间线按company/event_time/id；自选按security保存、公司页可聚合显示。原型仅固定样例，不实现这些查询。

## 7. 本轮验证边界

字段目录已覆盖逻辑对象和核心页面写入；引用存在性/字段唯一性/唯一键字段检查由生成工具执行。未连接数据库，未生成或执行DDL，不报告FK/索引/事务/锁/权限实际通过。实现时将字段设计转为Alembic迁移，按切片执行既有T测试；生产类型容量/索引性能由真实工作负载确认。

/* Local design prototype. Fixed synthetic snapshots; no API, persistence or rule engine. */
'use strict';
const $ = (s) => document.querySelector(s);
const esc = (v) => String(v ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const badge=(text,tone='gray')=>`<span class="badge ${tone}">${esc(text)}</span>`;
const btn=(text,action,cls='secondary',extra='')=>`<button type="button" class="${cls}" data-action="${action}" ${extra}>${text}</button>`;
const notice=(title,body,tone='')=>`<div class="notice ${tone}"><div><strong>${title}</strong><p>${body}</p></div></div>`;
const header=(title,sub,actions='')=>`<div class="page-header"><div><h1>${title}</h1><p>${sub}</p></div><div class="actions">${actions}</div></div>`;
const panel=(title,body,sub='',action='')=>`<section class="panel"><div class="panel-head"><div><h2>${title}</h2>${sub?`<p>${sub}</p>`:''}</div>${action}</div><div class="panel-body">${body}</div></section>`;
const link=(route,text)=>`<a href="#${route}">${text}</a>`;
const roles={viewer:['research.read'],researcher:['research.read','analysis.override','template.edit'],strategy_manager:['research.read','template.edit','template.publish','strategy.publish'],data_admin:['research.read','source.manage','job.retry','identity.manage','quality.correct'],system_admin:['role.assign','model.configure','audit.read','ops.read']};
roles.lead=[...new Set([...roles.researcher,...roles.strategy_manager])];
let state;
const initial=()=>({mode:'business',role:'lead',scenario:'normal',company:'a',tab:'timeline',market:'all',changeType:'all',query:'',watch:['a-a'],note:'',decisionKind:'impact',replacement:'-0.2',reason:'',judgmentStatus:'auto',generation:3,decisionOriginal:'+0.8',grade:'1',decisionRecords:{impact:{value:'+0.8',status:'auto',generation:3},rubric:{value:'3',status:'auto',generation:2},risk:{value:'已接受，目标仅SYN-H挂牌',status:'auto',generation:1}},templateAge:'90',eventMode:'enabled',templateDraft:false,templatePreview:false,templatePublished:false,strategyThreshold:'70',strategyPreview:false,strategyPublished:false,sourcePaused:false,imported:false,jobRecovered:false,roleChanges:false,correctionAdded:false});
state=initial();
const companies={a:{name:'示例制造',sector:'一般制造',quality:'72.0',coverage:'100%',securities:[{id:'a-a',name:'SYN-A',market:'A 股',currency:'CNY',v:'68.0',price:'12.40',state:'待评估 · 最后区间内',tone:'amber'},{id:'a-h',name:'SYN-H',market:'港股',currency:'HKD',v:'45.0',price:'18.60',state:'区间外',tone:'gray'}]},b:{name:'示例设备',sector:'一般制造',quality:'76.0',coverage:'100%',securities:[{id:'b-a',name:'SYN-B',market:'A 股',currency:'CNY',v:'64.0',price:'16.20',state:'待入选 1/2',tone:'blue'}]}};
companies.a.dimensions=[['profit_quality','盈利质量','76.0','0.35'],['financial_resilience','财务韧性','75.0','0.20'],['business_model','商业模式','70.0','0.25'],['governance','治理与资本配置','65.0','0.15'],['growth_sustainability','成长持续性','63.0','0.05']];
companies.b.dimensions=[['profit_quality','盈利质量','80.0','0.30'],['financial_resilience','财务韧性','78.0','0.20'],['business_model','商业模式','74.0','0.25'],['governance','治理与资本配置','72.0','0.15'],['growth_sustainability','成长持续性','71.0','0.10']];
const changes=[{id:'enter',type:'ENTER',label:'新入选',tone:'green',company:'a',security:'SYN-A',market:'a',date:'09-29',title:'两个相邻收盘时点评估均满足入选条件',body:'经营分 72.0，估值分 68.0；规则与证据已固定。'}, {id:'risk',type:'RISK',label:'挂牌风险',tone:'red',company:'a',security:'SYN-H',market:'h',date:'09-30',title:'合成H挂牌退市决定，作用于这只挂牌证券',body:'SYN-A 不受该挂牌风险影响；H证券保持区间外。'}, {id:'missing',type:'MISSING',label:'资料缺口',tone:'amber',company:'a',security:'SYN-A',market:'a',date:'09-30',title:'本交易日最终收盘价尚未取得',body:'当前待评估，保留最后已确认的区间内状态。'}, {id:'config',type:'CONFIG_CHANGE',label:'规则变更',tone:'blue',company:'b',security:'SYN-B',market:'a',date:'09-28',title:'新策略版本建立基线',body:'配置变化单独记录，不作为市场进入或退出。'}];
const can=(cap)=>roles[state.role]?.includes(cap);
const isPhone=()=>window.matchMedia('(max-width:750px)').matches;
const navs={business:[['changes','◷','今日变化'],['companies','▤','公司研究'],['strategy','◇','策略候选'],['watchlist','☆','我的自选']],admin:[['sources','▥','数据源'],['jobs','≋','运行任务'],['quality','△','数据质量'],['identity','▦','公司与证券'],['decisions','◎','分析复核'],['templates','▧','评分模板'],['strategy','◇','策略发布'],['models','◈','模型与费用'],['roles','♙','用户与权限'],['audit','≡','审计']]};
const routes={changes:renderChanges,change:renderChange,companies:renderCompanies,company:renderCompany,evidence:renderEvidence,decisions:renderDecision,templates:renderTemplates,strategy:renderStrategy,watchlist:renderWatchlist,sources:renderSources,jobs:renderJobs,quality:renderQuality,identity:renderIdentity,models:renderModels,roles:renderRoles,audit:renderAudit};
function toast(t){$('#toast').textContent=t;$('#toast').style.display='block';clearTimeout(toast.timer);toast.timer=setTimeout(()=>$('#toast').style.display='none',3000);}
function dialog(html){$('#dialog-content').innerHTML=html;$('#dialog').showModal();}
function navigate(route){if(location.hash==='#'+route)render();else location.hash=route;}
function permission(title,body){return `<div class="panel empty"><h2>${title}</h2><p>${body}</p>${link('changes','回到今日变化')}</div>`;}
function currentRoute(){return (location.hash.slice(1)||'changes').split('/');}
function render(){
 const [route,arg]=currentRoute(); if(arg && ['company','decisions'].includes(route))state.company=arg==='b'?'b':'a';
 document.body.dataset.mode=state.mode;
 $('#nav').innerHTML=navs[state.mode].map(([path,icon,name])=>`<a class="nav-link ${route===path||(path==='companies'&&route==='company')?'active':''}" href="#${path}"><span class="nav-icon" aria-hidden="true">${icon}</span>${name}</a>`).join('');
 $('#business-mode').classList.toggle('selected',state.mode==='business');$('#admin-mode').classList.toggle('selected',state.mode==='admin');$('#role').value=state.role;
 const business=['changes','change','companies','company','evidence','watchlist','decisions','templates','strategy','identity'];
 let html=business.includes(route)&&!can('research.read')?permission('此身份没有研究资料查看权限','系统管理员可管理用户和连接，不隐式获得研究判断或策略发布权限。可从上方切换合成身份查看另一条用户路径。'):(routes[route]||renderChanges)(arg);
 const scenario=state.scenario;
 if(scenario==='loading')html=header('正在取得研究快照','保留页面结构，等待资料加载。')+panel('研究资料','<div class="skeleton"></div><div class="skeleton large"></div>'+btn('完成合成加载','finish-load'));
 else if(scenario==='empty')html=header('没有符合条件的结果','当前筛选范围没有可展示的记录。')+`<div class="panel empty"><h2>试试扩大筛选范围</h2><p>这里表示筛选无结果。尚未接入、资料不可见和确实没有变化是不同状态。</p>${btn('清除筛选','finish-load','primary')}</div>`;
 else if(scenario==='permission')html=permission('当前对象不可查看','可见范围已变化，页面不显示原文、分数或计数。请联系本组织管理员检查授权。');
 else if(scenario==='partial')html=notice('部分资料取得失败','示例制造的原文和经营分仍可查看；本日A股估值缺价，无法作新的入选判断。'+link('quality','查看受影响范围'))+html;
 if(scenario==='updated')html=notice('有新的研究结果','你正在看的快照继续保留。查看新结果后再更新页面，不突然替换正在阅读的内容。','info')+btn('查看新快照','finish-load')+html;
 if(scenario==='suspended')html=notice('证券已停牌','SYN-A 显示暂停并保留上次已确认状态；恢复后需重新确认，不产生重复进入记录。')+html;
 $('#main').innerHTML=html+`<div class="demo-controls"><strong>设计状态演示</strong><label for="scenario">页面状态</label><select id="scenario"><option value="normal">正常</option><option value="loading">加载</option><option value="empty">筛选无结果</option><option value="partial">部分失败</option><option value="permission">不可见</option><option value="conflict">编辑冲突</option><option value="missing">UNKNOWN / 重算等待</option><option value="updated">新版本提示</option><option value="suspended">停牌</option></select><span>原型状态用于讨论交互，不代表服务端验收。</span></div>`;
 $('#main').dataset.route=location.hash||'#changes';
 $('#scenario').value=state.scenario; $('#scenario').addEventListener('change',e=>{state.scenario=e.target.value;render();});
 bindForms();
}
function renderChanges(){
 const rows=changes.filter(c=>(state.market==='all'||c.market===state.market)&&(state.changeType==='all'||c.type===state.changeType));
 return header('今日变化','先看变化，再回到当时的规则和证据。')+`<div class="toolbar"><div class="filters"><label>日期 <select aria-label="日期"><option>2026-09-28 至 09-30 · 演练窗口</option></select></label>${['all','a','h'].map((x,i)=>btn(['全部市场','A 股','港股'][i],'market-'+x,'filter-button '+(state.market===x?'active':''))).join('')}</div><span class="quiet">演练包含 09-28 至 09-30 的固定快照</span></div><div class="summary-line"><div><strong>1</strong><span>新入选证券</span></div><div><strong>1</strong><span>明确挂牌风险</span></div><div><strong>1</strong><span>资料缺口</span></div><div><strong>2</strong><span>关注公司</span></div></div><div class="toolbar"><h2>值得查看的变化</h2><div class="filters"><label>变化类型 <select id="change-filter"><option value="all">全部变化</option><option value="ENTER">新入选</option><option value="RISK">风险</option><option value="MISSING">资料缺口</option><option value="CONFIG_CHANGE">规则变更</option></select></label></div></div><section class="panel">${rows.map(c=>`<article class="change-row"><div class="date">${c.date}<br>固定历史快照</div><div>${badge(c.label,c.tone)}<h3 style="margin-top:8px">${companies[c.company].name}</h3><p>${c.security}</p></div><div><h3>${c.title}</h3><p>${c.body}</p></div>${link('change/'+c.id,'查看依据')}</article>`).join('')||'<div class="empty">当前筛选没有变化。</div>'}</section><div class="flow-link">${link('company/a','从公司展开研究')}${link('decisions/a','查看自动判断与人工覆盖')}${link('strategy','查看策略候选')}</div>`;
}
function renderChange(id='enter'){
 const c=changes.find(x=>x.id===id)||changes[0];const isRisk=c.type==='RISK';const missing=c.type==='MISSING';
 return `<div class="breadcrumb">${link('changes','今日变化')} / 固定历史解释</div>`+header(c.title,`${companies[c.company].name}　${c.security}　记录日期 2026-${c.date}`,badge(c.label,c.tone))+notice('你正在看当时记录',isRisk?'本条只针对SYN-H主挂牌。拒绝此风险不会解除另一个公司级风险。':missing?'当日最终收盘价缺失，显示待评估，最后确认状态仍是区间内。':'当时规则和输入已经固定；后续模板、判断和价格变化不会改写本条解释。','info')+`<div class="split">${panel('当时为什么发生变化',`<dl class="definition"><dt>策略发布</dt><dd>标准价投候选 · 发布 1</dd><dt>证券范围</dt><dd>${c.security}，${isRisk?'只适用H挂牌':'按本证券市场独立评估'}</dd><dt>前后状态</dt><dd>${isRisk?'区间外 → 区间外，新增风险原因':missing?'区间内 → 待评估，保留已确认区间内':'区间外 → 区间内'}</dd><dt>经营分 / 覆盖</dt><dd>72.0 / 100%</dd><dt>估值分</dt><dd>${isRisk?'45.0':missing?'无有效资料':'68.0'}</dd><dt>依据</dt><dd>${link('evidence','查看合成原始披露')}</dd></dl>${!isRisk&&!missing?'<ul class="reason-list"><li>公司经营分 72.0 ≥ 70<span>覆盖度100%，必需维度有效</span></li><li>本证券估值分 68.0 ≥ 60<span>A股价格与同口径财务匹配，不使用H股价格</span></li><li>两个相邻有效FINAL session均满足<span>09-28、09-29；不是两次页面刷新</span></li></ul>':''}`)}${panel('查看当前与更正',`<p>历史解释回答“当时为什么”；公司档案回答“现在是什么”。</p><div class="actions">${link('company/'+c.company,'查看当前公司状态')}${link('quality','查看更正与资料缺口')}</div><div class="focus-note">${isRisk?'H挂牌退市不等于公司全部证券退市。':'后来修正s2的输入，也需要检查s4、s6后续依据，不直接改今天的状态。'}</div>`,'固定解释与当前状态分开')}</div>`;
}
function renderCompanies(){
 const list=Object.entries(companies).filter(([,c])=>!state.query||c.name.includes(state.query)||c.securities.some(s=>s.name.toLowerCase().includes(state.query.toLowerCase())));
 return header('公司研究','列表按经营主体组织。证券候选与估值在公司内按 A/H 分开。')+`<div class="toolbar"><label class="filters">查找 <input id="company-search" type="search" aria-label="查找公司" placeholder="公司名或 SYN-A" value="${esc(state.query)}"></label><span class="quiet">两家公司 · 三只合成证券</span></div><section class="panel table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>公司 / 行业</th><th>经营分</th><th>覆盖度</th><th>证券与状态</th><th>最近变化</th><th>研究</th></tr></thead><tbody>${list.map(([id,c])=>`<tr><td>${link('company/'+id,c.name)}<p>${c.sector}</p></td><td class="number">${c.quality}</td><td>${c.coverage}</td><td>${c.securities.map(s=>`${s.name} ${badge(s.state,s.tone)}`).join('<br>')}</td><td>${id==='a'?'H挂牌风险，A报价缺口':'连续确认 1/2'}</td><td>${link('company/'+id,'打开档案')}</td></tr>`).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="6">没有匹配公司，请修改搜索词。</td></tr>'}</tbody></table></section>`;
}
function renderCompany(id='a'){
 const c=companies[state.company];const missing=state.scenario==='missing'||state.judgmentStatus==='waiting';const tabs=['timeline','scores','finance','valuation','relations','notes'];const names=['时间线','评分解释','财务','证券估值','关系','笔记'];
 let body='';
 if(state.tab==='timeline')body=`<div class="wide-split">${panel('公司事件时间线',`<div class="timeline-row"><div class="time-label">09-30<br>发生时间</div><div>${badge('仅H挂牌风险','red')}<h3 style="margin-top:8px">合成H挂牌退市决定</h3><p>证据明确指向SYN-H主挂牌，SYN-A不因这一风险改变状态。</p>${link('evidence','查看原始披露')}<div class="actions">${btn('查看风险判断','risk-detail','text-button')}</div></div></div><div class="timeline-row"><div class="time-label">09-29<br>系统得知</div><div>${badge(state.judgmentStatus==='auto'?'自动接受':'人工覆盖',state.judgmentStatus==='auto'?'blue':'amber')}<h3 style="margin-top:8px">同一订单事实的多份材料已归并</h3><p>两份合成材料支持同一经济事实，只计一次影响。${state.judgmentStatus==='auto'?'自动影响判断为 +0.8。':'人工新判断已记录；依赖重算状态单独展示。'}</p><div class="actions">${link('evidence','展开支持与反驳证据')}${link('decisions/'+state.company,'查看 / 修改影响')}</div></div></div><div class="timeline-row"><div class="time-label">09-30<br>报告期间</div><div><h3>财务资料与治理rubric基准更新</h3><p>已吸收订单事实的基准不再叠加同一事件贡献。</p>${btn('查看经营分依据','tab-scores','text-button')}</div></div>`)}${panel('本次研究要点',`<ul class="reason-list"><li>公司经营分独立于挂牌价格<span>${c.quality}分，覆盖${c.coverage}</span></li><li>A/H估值与候选状态不同<span>SYN-A估值68.0；SYN-H估值45.0</span></li><li>自动判断可以被有权人员修正<span>无需逐项人工审批才能生效</span></li></ul>${link('templates','查看使用的三层模板')}`)}</div>`;
 else if(state.tab==='scores')body=panel('经营分如何形成',`<div class="split"><div>${c.dimensions.map(([dimension,n,v,w])=>`<div class="dimension-row"><span>${n}<small class="quiet"> ${Math.round(Number(w)*100)}%</small></span><div class="bar"><i style="width:${v}%"></i></div><div class="value">${Number(v).toFixed(1)}</div></div>`).join('')}<p class="footnote">条形图为固定合成样例，各字段从同一解释快照展示；页面没有评分引擎。</p></div><div><h3>展开治理与资本配置</h3><dl class="definition"><dt>原始基准</dt><dd>rubric及档位证据</dd><dt>事件贡献</dt><dd>按经济事实去重、半衰期和上限</dd><dt>口径来源</dt><dd>基础 → 制造业 → 企业差异</dd></dl><div class="actions">${btn('修订rubric档位','rubric-detail')}${link('templates','比较继承与覆盖')}</div></div></div>`,'总分、覆盖与缺口并列，不把未知显示为0');
 else if(state.tab==='valuation')body=panel('按证券查看估值',`<div class="tabs">${c.securities.map(s=>btn(s.name+' / '+s.market,'valuation-'+s.id,'filter-button '+(state.selectedSecurity===s.id?'active':''))).join('')}</div>${valuation(c.securities.find(s=>s.id===state.selectedSecurity)||c.securities[0])}`,'公司经营事实共享，报价、币种、分母与V独立');
 else if(state.tab==='finance')body=panel('财务与报告义务',`<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>指标</th><th>规范值</th><th>期间/口径</th><th>依据</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>ROE TTM</td><td>12.0%</td><td>普通股归属利润 / 匹配权益</td><td>${link('evidence','定位表格单元')}</td></tr><tr><td>3年CFO / 净利润</td><td>0.96</td><td>三完整年度合并金额先求和再相除</td><td>${link('evidence','查看三年依据')}</td></tr><tr><td>净债务 / EBITDA</td><td>1.20</td><td>同口径、同币种；分母有效</td><td>${link('evidence','查看指标出处')}</td></tr></tbody></table></div><div class="focus-note">下一份应有报告按冻结报告义务判断。三年历史指标需要旧年度资料，不统一套180日过期。</div>`);
 else if(state.tab==='relations')body=panel('主体与挂牌关系',`<h3>${c.name}（经营主体）</h3><ul class="reason-list">${c.securities.map(s=>`<li>${s.name} / ${s.market}<span>${s.currency}报价，独立工具身份及主要挂牌；经营资料关联同一公司</span></li>`).join('')}</ul><p class="footnote">关系传播需要有效期和依据，不沿母子公司无限扩散。</p>${link('identity','查看主数据关系与来源')}`);
 else body=panel('研究笔记',`<form id="notes-form"><div class="form-group"><label for="note">我的私人笔记</label><textarea id="note" placeholder="记录你的研究疑问，默认仅本人可见">${esc(state.note)}</textarea><small>本原型只保存于内存；正式设计使用服务端修订与If-Match。</small></div>${'<button type="submit" class="primary">保存笔记</button>'}</form>`);
 if(state.company==='b'&&state.tab==='timeline')body=panel('示例设备事件时间线','<div class="timeline-row"><div class="time-label">09-29</div><div><h3>合成年度资料更新</h3><p>仅发行SYN-B一只A股证券。经营分76.0，估值64.0；当前相邻确认1/2。</p></div></div><p class="footnote">示例制造的H挂牌风险与企业模板差异不适用于本公司。</p>');
 return `<div class="breadcrumb">${link('companies','公司研究')} / ${c.name}</div>`+header(`<span class="company-identity"><span class="company-monogram">${c.name.slice(2,3)}</span><span>${c.name}<small>${c.sector}　经营主体 · 合成公司</small></span></span>`,(state.company==='a'?'经营分快照09-29；09-30 A报价缺口。历史记录与当前状态分别查看。':'经营分与估值快照09-29；当前相邻确认1/2。'),btn(state.watch.includes(c.securities[0].id)?'已加入自选':'加入自选','watch-toggle'))+(missing?notice('新判断已记录，依赖结果正在重算','旧经营分快照仅供历史比较，当前为待评估，不能把旧贡献当作新值已生效。'):'')+`<div class="split">${panel('公司经营质量',`<div class="score-overview"><div><div class="score-number">${missing?'—':c.quality}</div><p>${missing?'待评估':'经营分'}</p></div><div><strong>覆盖 ${missing?'待更新':c.coverage}</strong><p>分数与覆盖度必须一起看</p>${link('templates','查看评分口径')}</div></div>`)}${panel('证券状态',c.securities.map(s=>`<div class="toolbar" style="margin:8px 0"><div><strong>${s.name}</strong><small class="quiet">　${s.market} / ${s.currency}</small></div>${badge(missing?'待评估':s.state,missing?'amber':s.tone)}</div>`).join('')+'<p class="footnote">'+(state.company==='a'?'A/H估值独立；H挂牌风险不会传播到A证券。':'本公司仅SYN-B证券，企业模板差异不影响其他公司。')+'</p>')}</div><div class="tabs">${tabs.map((x,i)=>btn(names[i],'tab-'+x,state.tab===x?'active':'')).join('')}</div>${body}`;
}
function valuation(s){return `<dl class="definition"><dt>证券 / 币种</dt><dd>${s.name} / ${s.currency}</dd><dt>正式收盘</dt><dd>${s.price} ${s.currency}　2026-09-29</dd><dt>估值分</dt><dd><strong style="font-size:30px">${s.v}</strong></dd><dt>分母口径</dt><dd>同权普通股每股归属盈利；含冻结股本与FX引用</dd><dt>行情状态</dt><dd>${badge('该session最终价','green')} 不等于评估已封存</dd><dt>依据</dt><dd>${link('evidence','查看财务、行情和单位依据')}</dd></dl>`;}
function renderEvidence(){state.company='a';return `<div class="breadcrumb">${link('company/'+state.company,'公司研究')} / 原始证据</div>`+header('合成披露：订单与挂牌事项','出处、定位和支持范围与判断一起阅读。',link('decisions/'+state.company,'查看对应判断'))+`<div class="split">${panel('原文与定位',`<div class="document"><div class="document-meta">来源：本地合成披露源<br>发布时间 2026-09-29 15:55　系统获取 16:40　正文定位 第2段<br>材料修订：演练修订2　支持/反驳证据各自保留</div><p>以下为专用于交互设计的虚构披露：</p><p>示例制造披露一项订单安排，<mark>订单以分期执行和客户验收为条件，尚不能确认全部转化为利润</mark>。本条用于比较原自动影响 +0.8 与研究员修订 −0.2 的证据解释。</p><p>另一个独立合成监管决定仅指向<mark>SYN-H主要挂牌</mark>，未指向公司所有证券。不得从来源市场推断风险作用范围。</p><p>本材料包含支持与限制性事实。模型摘要不替代这段可定位原文。</p></div>`)}${panel('证据与判断',`<ul class="reason-list"><li>公司关联：示例制造<span>直接主体；关联度与判断可信程度独立</span></li><li>订单事实影响<span>同一经济事实，即使有多篇转载也只计一次贡献</span></li><li>挂牌风险<span>目标为SYN-H listing；传播到确切证券</span></li></ul><div class="actions">${link('decisions/a','修改影响判断')}${btn('查看挂牌风险','risk-detail')}</div>${btn('演示证据不可访问','evidence-revoke','text-button')}`)}</div>`;}
function renderDecision(){
 state.company='a';
 const kind=state.decisionKind;const impact=kind==='impact';const rubric=kind==='rubric';const risk=kind==='risk';const permitted=can('analysis.override');
 const label=impact?'订单事实 · 盈利质量影响':rubric?'资本配置 · rubric档位':'SYN-H挂牌退市风险';
 const record=state.decisionRecords[kind];const old=record.value;
 const newValue=impact?state.replacement:rubric?state.grade:'拒绝本风险';
 const waiting=record.status==='waiting'||state.scenario==='missing';
 return `<div class="breadcrumb">${link('company/'+state.company,'公司时间线')} / 判断详情与人工覆盖</div>`+header(label,'查看原文、旧值与完整新值，明确作用对象后直接保存。',badge(record.status==='auto'?'AUTO accepted':record.status==='released'?'覆盖已解除，等待重新评估':'HUMAN 有效覆盖',record.status==='auto'?'blue':'amber'))+`<div class="tabs">${['impact','rubric','risk'].map((x,i)=>btn(['影响值','rubric档位','硬风险'][i],'decision-'+x,kind===x?'active':'')).join('')}</div>`+(state.scenario==='conflict'?notice('判断已被另一位研究员更新','你的新值和理由保留在下方。当前服务器示例值已变化，比较后可基于新版本重试。','error'):'')+(waiting?notice('人工判断已保存，重算等待中','新判断优先于后续AUTO建议；公司评分和A/H评估尚未完成。'+(risk?'若仍有公司级违约，两只证券继续受风险影响。':''),'info'):'')+`<div class="split">${panel('原文依据',`<p>订单分期执行，需经客户验收，不能确认全部转化为利润。</p><div class="focus-note">${risk?'监管决定明确指向SYN-H主要挂牌；不存在把同公司A证券一并退市的依据。':rubric?'rubric档位按固定锚点与证据选择，未知不填中位数。':'修改影响是修改经营判断，不是预测股价。'}</div>${link('evidence','打开完整原文与证据定位')}<dl class="definition"><dt>主体</dt><dd>示例制造</dd><dt>作用范围</dt><dd>${risk?'仅SYN-H主挂牌':'公司经营评分；A/H共享经营事实，各自估值'}</dd><dt>生效方式</dt><dd>从本次实际知悉时点生效，不回填系统历史</dd><dt>有效期</dt><dd>合成演练至2026-10-31；到期固定cutoff重评</dd><dt>输入证据</dt><dd>本页合成披露第2段</dd></dl>`)}${panel('人工覆盖',`<div class="value-compare"><div class="value-box"><span>当前有效值</span><strong>${esc(old)}</strong><small>${state.scenario==='conflict'?'冲突：已有更新版本':'当前判断版本 '+record.generation}</small></div><div class="value-box"><span>准备修改为</span><strong>${esc(newValue)}</strong><small>新修订保留旧值与原因</small></div></div>${!permitted?notice('此角色只能查看','查看者、策略管理员与系统管理员不隐式拥有analysis.override。可切换研究员身份体验。'):''}<form id="override-form"><div class="form-group"><label for="replacement">${impact?'完整新影响值（−1 至 +1）':rubric?'新档位（0 至 4）':'明确动作'}</label>${impact?`<input id="replacement" name="replacement" type="number" min="-1" max="1" step="0.1" value="${esc(state.replacement)}" ${!permitted?'disabled':''}>`:rubric?`<select id="grade" ${!permitted?'disabled':''}>${[0,1,2,3,4].map(g=>`<option ${String(g)===state.grade?'selected':''}>${g}</option>`).join('')}</select>`:'<p>拒绝这一条确切挂牌风险；不按risk_code全公司清除。</p>'}<small>证据、主体、事实时期及其他完整字段沿用本页固定样例。改身份应新建判断。</small></div><div class="form-group"><label for="reason">修改理由</label><textarea id="reason" required placeholder="说明证据为什么支持新判断" ${!permitted?'disabled':''}>${esc(state.reason)}</textarea></div><div id="override-error" class="form-error" role="alert"></div><div class="actions"><button type="submit" class="primary" ${!permitted?'disabled':''}>${risk?'拒绝本风险':'保存人工覆盖'}</button></div></form>${record.status!=='auto'&&record.status!=='released'?`<div class="actions">${btn('查看合成重算结果','show-recompute')}${btn('解除人工覆盖','release-override','text-button',!permitted?'disabled':'')}</div>`:''}<p class="footnote">单项编辑不增加预览审批；正式服务验证If-Match与幂等键。原型仅演示交互。</p>`,'当前值、新值和证据并列')}</div>`;
}

function renderStrategy(){
 const allowed=can('strategy.publish')&&!isPhone();
 const rows=Object.values(companies).flatMap(c=>c.securities.map(s=>({c,s})));
 return header('标准价投候选','按证券评估；公司经营分与证券估值分分别展示。',badge(state.strategyPublished?'演示发布 2':'发布 1 · 固定口径','blue'))+`<section class="panel table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>公司 / 证券</th><th>经营分 / 覆盖</th><th>估值分</th><th>策略状态</th><th>关键原因</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows.map(({c,s})=>`<tr><td>${link('company/'+(c.name==='示例制造'?'a':'b'),c.name)}<p>${s.name} / ${s.market}</p></td><td>${c.quality} / ${c.coverage}</td><td class="number">${s.v}</td><td>${badge(s.state,s.tone)}</td><td>${s.id==='a-h'?'V低于进入阈值，另有挂牌风险':s.id==='b-a'?'首个有效确认；1/2':'两相邻FINAL满足条件'}<p>${link(s.id==='a-h'?'change/risk':'change/enter','查看规则与依据')}</p></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></section>${panel('维护策略草稿',`<div class="form-grid"><div class="form-group"><label for="strategy-threshold">进入经营分阈值</label><select id="strategy-threshold" ${!allowed?'disabled':''}><option ${state.strategyThreshold==='70'?'selected':''}>70</option><option ${state.strategyThreshold==='75'?'selected':''}>75</option></select><small>退出维持阈值65、其他条件沿用固定样例。只提供有对照快照的两个演示选项。</small></div><div><h3>条件的业务解释</h3><p>经营分 ≥ ${state.strategyThreshold}，覆盖 ≥ 80%，证券估值 ≥ 60；财务指标与风险门保持。</p><p class="footnote">两相邻expected FINAL确认；UNKNOWN打断pending。</p></div></div>${!allowed?notice(isPhone()?'手机查看模式':'当前身份没有策略编辑/发布能力',isPhone()?'复杂规则编辑、模拟和发布请到桌面完成。':'研究员可覆盖判断，不能发布策略。切换策略管理员可体验模拟发布。'):''}<div class="actions">${btn('固定时点模拟','simulate-strategy','primary',!allowed?'disabled':'')}</div>`,'草稿改动使旧预览失效，发布不改旧版')}${state.strategyPreview?panel('模拟变化预览',`<div class="steps"><div class="step active"><strong>固定时点</strong><span>2026-09-29收盘输入</span></div><div class="step active"><strong>比较变化</strong><span>${state.strategyThreshold==='75'?'示例制造72不再满足进入条件':'沿用原进入阈值'}</span></div><div class="step"><strong>发布新基线</strong><span>标记CONFIG_CHANGE</span></div></div><table><thead><tr><th>证券</th><th>旧规则</th><th>新规则基线</th></tr></thead><tbody><tr><td>SYN-A</td><td>区间内</td><td>${state.strategyThreshold==='75'?'区间外 · CONFIG_CHANGE':'区间内 · 原规则符合'}</td></tr><tr><td>SYN-H</td><td>区间外</td><td>区间外 · 独立挂牌风险</td></tr><tr><td>SYN-B</td><td>待确认</td><td>新release按有效输入静默建基线，不生成市场ENTER</td></tr></tbody></table><p class="footnote">固定样例模拟结果，无实时策略计算；发布只更新原型版本标签。</p><div class="actions">${btn('确认发布新版本','publish-strategy','primary',!allowed?'disabled':'')}</div>`):''}${panel('发布历史与回滚','<p>发布1继续保留固定规则和历史解释。回滚应引用旧配置创建新release并重新预览，不覆盖发布1。</p>'+btn('查看回滚预览','rollback-preview','text-button',!allowed?'disabled':''))}`;
}
function renderWatchlist(){const securities=Object.entries(companies).flatMap(([id,c])=>c.securities.map(s=>({id,c,s}))).filter(x=>state.watch.includes(x.s.id));return header('我的自选','按证券保存，研究时可以回到同一公司档案。')+panel('关注证券',securities.length?`<div class="table-wrap"><table><thead><tr><th>公司 / 证券</th><th>经营分</th><th>证券状态</th><th>操作</th></tr></thead><tbody>${securities.map(({id,c,s})=>`<tr><td>${link('company/'+id,c.name)}<p>${s.name}</p></td><td>${c.quality}</td><td>${badge(s.state,s.tone)}</td><td>${btn('移出自选','unwatch-'+s.id,'text-button')}</td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`:`<div class="empty"><h2>开始关注一个研究对象</h2><p>在公司档案选择“加入自选”。</p>${link('companies','查找公司')}</div>`);}
function adminPermission(cap,title){return !can(cap)?permission('此身份没有'+title+'权限','请切换对应合成角色查看管理交互。管理权限与业务覆盖/发布分别授予。'):null;}
function renderSources(){const denied=adminPermission('source.manage','数据源管理');if(denied)return denied;return header('数据源','先看采集范围、最近成功与业务影响，再展开技术诊断。')+panel('本地合成披露源',`<div class="toolbar"><h3>local-fixture</h3>${badge(state.sourcePaused?'已暂停':'已启用',state.sourcePaused?'amber':'green')}</div><dl class="definition"><dt>业务范围</dt><dd>两家公司，三只证券</dd><dt>权利状态</dt><dd>合成fixture，可读/保存/分析；不配置真实凭证</dd><dt>最近运行</dt><dd>${state.imported?'演示导入完成':'等待演示导入'}</dd><dt>下一计划</dt><dd>${state.sourcePaused?'暂停后不再创建run':'固定演练计划，不实际调度'}</dd></dl><div class="actions">${btn(state.imported?'查看导入结果':'导入合成资料','import-fixture','primary',state.sourcePaused?'disabled':'')}${btn(state.sourcePaused?'恢复计划':'暂停计划','toggle-source')}${link('jobs','查看采集任务')}</div>`)+panel('频率与范围说明','<p>真实源适配时再固定供应商、抓取/保存/模型分析/导出权利。配置发布可先预览请求量与回溯范围；常规草稿不逐项审批。</p>'+btn('查看配置发布预览','source-preview','text-button'));}
function renderJobs(){const denied=!(can('job.retry')||can('ops.read'));if(denied)return permission('仅运行管理身份可查看任务','数据管理员可以恢复失败项，系统管理员可以查看运行诊断。');return header('运行任务','失败项与受影响公司一起显示，不把失败冒充没有资料。')+panel('合成行情更新',`<div class="toolbar"><h3>SYN-A 正式收盘价</h3>${badge(state.jobRecovered?'演示恢复完成':'一项失败',state.jobRecovered?'green':'amber')}</div><dl class="definition"><dt>影响</dt><dd>${state.jobRecovered?'已取得固定样例，等待策略重评':'SYN-A 当前待评估，保留最后已确认区间内'}</dd><dt>原因</dt><dd>合成源本session暂缺最终价</dd><dt>恢复范围</dt><dd>只恢复这一session失败项，不重跑membership或历史转移</dd><dt>账本</dt><dd>沿用完整逻辑键与cutoff，丢ACK不重复生效</dd></dl><div class="actions">${btn('预览失败项恢复','retry-preview','primary',!can('job.retry')?'disabled':'')}${link('quality','查看业务影响')}${link('company/a','回到公司研究')}</div>`)+panel('导入与分析阶段','<div class="steps"><div class="step active"><strong>原文保存</strong><span>演示成功</span></div><div class="step active"><strong>判断保存</strong><span>自动接受 / 人工覆盖</span></div><div class="step"><strong>评分与评估</strong><span>按固定时点完成重算</span></div></div>');}

function bindForms(){
 const f=$('#change-filter');if(f){f.value=state.changeType;f.onchange=e=>{state.changeType=e.target.value;render();};}
 const search=$('#company-search');if(search)search.oninput=e=>{state.query=e.target.value;const p=e.target.selectionStart;render();const n=$('#company-search');n.focus();n.setSelectionRange(p,p);};
 const replacement=$('#replacement');if(replacement)replacement.oninput=e=>{state.replacement=e.target.value;};
 const grade=$('#grade');if(grade)grade.onchange=e=>{state.grade=e.target.value;};
 const reason=$('#reason');if(reason)reason.oninput=e=>{state.reason=e.target.value;};
 const form=$('#override-form');if(form)form.onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();if(!can('analysis.override'))return;const err=$('#override-error');if(!state.reason.trim()){err.textContent='请写明修改理由，便于回查证据。';return;}if(state.decisionKind==='impact'&&(state.replacement===''||!Number.isFinite(Number(state.replacement))||Number(state.replacement)<-1||Number(state.replacement)>1)){err.textContent='影响值须为−1至+1之间的数值。';return;}if(state.scenario==='conflict'){err.innerHTML='示例409：你的草稿保留。'+btn('比较并采用新版本重试','resolve-conflict','text-button');return;}const record=state.decisionRecords[state.decisionKind];record.value=state.decisionKind==='impact'?state.replacement:state.decisionKind==='rubric'?state.grade:'人工拒绝本挂牌风险';record.status='waiting';record.generation++;state.judgmentStatus='waiting';state.generation++;render();toast('合成人工修订已保存，依赖重算等待中');};
 const nform=$('#notes-form');if(nform)nform.onsubmit=e=>{e.preventDefault();state.note=$('#note').value;toast('笔记保存在本次原型内存中');};
 const age=$('#template-age');if(age)age.onchange=e=>{state.templateAge=e.target.value;state.templatePreview=false;state.templatePublished=false;render();};
 const mode=$('#event-mode');if(mode)mode.onchange=e=>{state.eventMode=e.target.value;state.templatePreview=false;render();};
 const threshold=$('#strategy-threshold');if(threshold)threshold.onchange=e=>{state.strategyThreshold=e.target.value;state.strategyPreview=false;render();};
 const rform=$('#roles-form');if(rform)rform.onsubmit=e=>e.preventDefault();
}
document.addEventListener('click',e=>{
 const el=e.target.closest('[data-action]');if(!el||el.disabled)return;const action=el.dataset.action;
 if(action.startsWith('tab-')){state.tab=action.slice(4);render();}
 else if(action.startsWith('market-')){state.market=action.slice(7);render();}
 else if(action.startsWith('decision-')){state.decisionKind=action.slice(9);render();}
 else if(action.startsWith('valuation-')){state.selectedSecurity=action.slice(10);render();}
 else if(action.startsWith('unwatch-')){state.watch=state.watch.filter(x=>x!==action.slice(8));render();}
 else if(action==='watch-toggle'){const id=companies[state.company].securities[0].id;state.watch=state.watch.includes(id)?state.watch.filter(x=>x!==id):[...state.watch,id];render();toast('自选更新仅保留在原型内存');}
 else if(action==='risk-detail'||action==='rubric-detail'){state.decisionKind=action==='risk-detail'?'risk':'rubric';navigate('decisions/'+state.company);}
 else if(action==='finish-load'){state.scenario='normal';state.market='all';state.changeType='all';render();}
 else if(action==='evidence-revoke'){dialog('<h2>原文现已不可访问</h2><p>权利已撤销或到期，保留引用与不可访问原因，不返回缓存正文。当前依赖评分需要重新检查覆盖度。</p><p class="footnote">此弹窗演示正式页面状态，不执行真实撤权。</p>');}
 else if(action==='show-recompute'){state.scenario='normal';dialog('<h2>合成重算结果对照</h2><p>新判断已记录为人工有效值。实际重算应返回同一快照的经营分、覆盖与A/H评估。</p><p>本原型没有Decimal评分或状态机，仅展示“判断已保存”与“计算已完成”两个阶段。</p>');}
 else if(action==='release-override'){if(!can('analysis.override'))return;state.decisionRecords[state.decisionKind].status='released';state.decisionRecords[state.decisionKind].value='无有效判断，待重评';state.judgmentStatus='waiting';state.generation++;dialog('<h2>人工覆盖已解除（合成）</h2><p>当前等待固定cutoff的新合法判断；不恢复过期AUTO。拒绝风险与解除覆盖是不同动作。</p>');render();}
 else if(action==='resolve-conflict'){state.scenario='normal';state.generation++;state.decisionRecords[state.decisionKind].generation++;render();toast('已载入新版本，你的新值和理由仍保留；可重试保存');}
 else if(action==='save-template'){if(!can('template.edit'))return;state.templateDraft=true;toast('合成模板草稿已保存，尚未发布');}
 else if(action==='preview-template'){if(!can('template.edit'))return;state.templatePreview=true;render();}
 else if(action==='restore-template'){state.templateAge='180';state.eventMode='enabled';state.templatePreview=false;render();}
 else if(action==='publish-template'){if(!can('template.publish')||!state.templatePreview)return;state.templatePublished=true;state.templatePreview=false;render();toast('演示新模板发布；旧release继续固定旧口径');}
 else if(action==='simulate-strategy'){if(!can('strategy.publish'))return;state.strategyPreview=true;render();}
 else if(action==='publish-strategy'){if(!can('strategy.publish')||!state.strategyPreview)return;state.strategyPublished=true;state.strategyPreview=false;render();toast('演示发布2，变化分类CONFIG_CHANGE');}
 else if(action==='rollback-preview'){dialog('<h2>回滚提案预览</h2><p>引用发布1配置，创建新的发布3；原发布2与历史解释保留。受影响证券仍为SYN-A、SYN-H、SYN-B。</p><p class="footnote">这里只查看设计提案，不执行发布。</p>');}
 else if(action==='toggle-source'){if(!can('source.manage'))return;state.sourcePaused=!state.sourcePaused;render();}
 else if(action==='import-fixture'){if(!can('source.manage'))return;state.imported=true;toast('合成导入结果演示已就绪，未运行采集');navigate('jobs');}
 else if(action==='source-preview'){dialog('<h2>配置发布预览</h2><p>范围为两家公司、三只证券；本地合成fixture，无网络请求和采购。正常接入时再明确回溯期、请求量、权利和恢复方法。</p>');}
 else if(action==='retry-preview'){if(!can('job.retry'))return;dialog('<h2>只恢复一个失败项</h2><p>对象：SYN-A / 2026-09-30最终行情。沿用固定cutoff和业务键，不重跑历史membership。</p><div class="actions">'+btn('确认合成恢复','confirm-retry','primary')+'</div>');}
 else if(action==='confirm-retry'){if(!can('job.retry'))return;state.jobRecovered=true;$('#dialog').close();render();toast('固定恢复结果已展示；未调用源或任务服务');}
 else if(action==='identity-preview'){dialog('<h2>主数据变更影响预览</h2><p>示例制造关联两只证券、一个策略release及当前研究判断。身份修改需要固定旧/新映射、generation和证据；历史引用保留。</p><p class="footnote">本轮只展示范围，未提出真实合并对象。</p>');}
 else if(action==='correction-preview'){if(!can('quality.correct'))return;dialog('<h2>历史更正范围</h2><p>s2原输入错误；s4/s6仍有独立有效依据。窗口重放后当前IN保持，追加CORRECTION说明。</p><div class="actions">'+btn('追加合成更正说明','confirm-correction','primary')+'</div>');}
 else if(action==='confirm-correction'){if(!can('quality.correct'))return;state.correctionAdded=true;$('#dialog').close();render();}
 else if(action==='role-preview'){if(!can('role.assign'))return;const checked=[...document.querySelectorAll('input[name="roles"]:checked')].map(x=>x.value);dialog('<h2>合成成员的权限差异</h2><p>当前：研究员。</p><p>准备设为：'+esc(checked.join('、')||'无业务角色')+'。</p><p>只改变这一名成员，下一请求和后台提交按新能力检查。此示例不移除最后管理员。</p><div class="actions">'+btn('保存合成角色组合','confirm-role','primary')+'</div>');}
 else if(action==='confirm-role'){if(!can('role.assign'))return;state.roleChanges=true;$('#dialog').close();toast('角色组合仅作原型演示，未修改真实权限');}
});

===== SOURCE: prototype/README.md =====

# 价值研究交互原型

直接打开[index.html](./index.html)。本地HTML/CSS/JavaScript，不需要安装依赖或启动产品服务；没有外部字体、图片、CDN、fetch/API请求。全部资料为两家合成公司、三只证券的固定交互样例。页面操作仅在内存中，刷新或“重置演示”清空。

路径：今日变化→当时解释→当前公司→原文→人工覆盖；公司评分/财务/A-H估值/笔记；三层模板→差异预览；策略候选→修改阈值→固定样例模拟→发布；管理模式的合成源→失败项恢复、质量更正、主体关系、用户角色、模型/费用与审计。

顶部“查看身份”切换五角色或研究员+策略管理员组合，仅为交互演示，不是真实认证。底部“设计状态演示”提供加载、空、部分失败、不可见、冲突、UNKNOWN/等待、新版本、停牌。改变策略阈值或模板设置会使旧预览失效。

所有评分/模拟输出为预先编写的对照材料，没有前端策略计算；不以原型保存/发布提示声称数据库写入。原型详解与需求连接见[16](../docs/16-prototype-and-design-trace.md)。产品实施与目标用户任务验收尚未执行。


===== SOURCE: review/V02-CHANGELOG.md =====

# v0.1 → v0.2 变更账本与证据边界

日期2026-09-30；旧基线`8b9d9de36d197b1932c4102cc98888d224984085`，本次为设计修订。用户已确认最终边界，工程代码、数据库、UI和真实接口尚未实施。旧版本由Git历史保留；当前权威入口是README与docs/11。

## 1. 用户决定

| 变更 | 原设计 | 当前设计 | 追踪 |
|---|---|---|---|
| 多人权限 | 一个私人组织，但访谈曾提出简化个人起步 | 首版五角色RBAC可兼任，前后端统一授权，不降为个人工具 | R-11/17，11 §5，T-20/29 |
| 三层模板 | 固定公司评分配置 | base→industry→company固定父版本、差异覆盖、有效配置/来源、升级预览 | R-05/15，11 §2/3，T-25/26 |
| 自动审核 | 高影响/硬风险先人工批准、人工基准 | DecisionService可AUTO接受关联/影响/rubric/硬风险，人工覆盖有效期间优先 | R-03/05/16，11 §4，T-27/28 |
| 外部模型 | 许可范围待确认 | 允许作为正常能力接入；来源配置与服务端基础权限，不堆复杂审批 | R-11/16，07/08/11 |
| 成本 | 开发和部署预算待决定 | 开发不设固定预算门，记录用量；真实供应商/资源采购后续选择 | 01/07/09 |
| 通知 | 首版站内主记录、订阅与提醒中心 | 当前生成可查策略变化与固定解释；全部收件人通知/订阅/投递归W-07后续 | R-08/09，02/05/06/09，T-13/16 |
| 技术范围 | 按小团队阶段交付 | 保留完整框架能力，分阶段只按依赖验证，不以技术复杂为由删需求 | R-13/14，09/13 |

## 2. Pro可见反馈与独立研究落实

完整Pro附件未取得，本表不宣称合并其60文件包；当前v0.2由本会话独立编写。第二轮报告保留对旧基线的研究事实，建议与当前合同冲突时以本表/11/12为准。

| 发现 | 当前修订 | 配置/合同影响 | 验收追踪 |
|---|---|---|---|
| B-01 财务/rubric | 合并CFO配合并净利润；11项0–4证据锚点 | metric-definitions-v2/rubrics/scoring Schema | T-09/10/36 |
| B-02 时间任务 | 时间相关job固定as_of/cutoff/session/版本；重试不换逻辑时点 | job-event Schema2.0/evaluation_context | T-01/08/18/35 |
| B-03 FINAL更正 | 更正窗口、历史CORRECTION、当前reconciliation分开 | correction记录/API/manifest | T-07/14/32 |
| B-04 旧备份撤权 | 旧DB外独立durable journal/latest watermark；恢复隔离 | 12 §7/08恢复合同 | T-15/19 |
| S-01 相邻session | 缺应有评估打断pending，休市不打断，旧session不倒灌 | strategy Schema2.0/FINAL token | T-13/30 |
| S-02 最终价/日历 | listing报价final标记，CAS/半日/覆盖不足/深市映射 | price/listing/calendar adapter | T-11/31 |
| S-03 输入时点分类 | 收盘价宽限可用，收盘后新公告不可入本次；判断不回填 | evaluation context与冻结manifest | T-07/38 |
| S-04 历史/当前分离 | 保留s2历史，独立s6仍合法时不抹当前IN | correction window +最新generation CAS | T-32 |

| S-06 新鲜度 | 按报告义务/所需期，旧更正不满足新期 | 移除financial_max_announced_age_days | T-09/37 |
| S-07 人审容量 | 用户选择自动生效，因此无需固定日人审；仍统计异常pending/覆盖负担 | AUTO政策、经济事实slot队列 | T-27/28/T-UX-01 |
| S-08 数值规范 | Decimal上下文/12位存储/运算顺序/UTC秒/显示分离 | numeric-policy-v1、scoring引用 | T-08/35 |
| S-09 Listing | company/security/listing明确定义，首版primary柜台固定 | 报价/V关联listing，membership仍security | T-10/11 |

其他采纳项：economic_fact贡献slot、baseline原子吸收、原文A→B→A observation、派生分数继承来源权限、服务端笔记/草稿/保存视图、对象清理核对DB活跃引用；对应T-33/34与11/12。

## 3. 开源取舍

继续模块化单体和PG业务真源；优先FastAPI全栈模板工程基座，Refine作为管理端候选不因TanStack而排除，Prefect作为Celery替代不因既有文档而排除。实际集成在W-08有界验证，不叠加同职能引擎。Qlib/LEAN隔离研究、FinanceToolkit公式对照、Scrapy获许可HTML适配；RQAlpha/AKShare许可和数据用途仍需分别核对。固定源码和观察证据见research/second-review.md，未安装或运行。

## 4. 一致性与未验证项

R-01…14/W-01…07/T-01…24保留，追加R-15…17/W-08/T-25…38及T-UX-04。首版通知范围按用户决定调整，T-16实际发送部分后续执行，不降低状态变化与去重验收。策略/评分文件名v1保持第一套标准配置名称，Schema/指标定义独立升版本，尚无生产配置被替换。

材料检查结果见validation-result.json：JSON、有限Schema形状、引用/hash、模板/规则一致性、合成黄金算术/负例等；不能替代完整JSON Schema库验证或产品运行验收。未执行数据库迁移/并发、真实源/模型、检索标注、UI/设备/UAT、性能和备份恢复；没有收益验证。GitHub提交与回读证据留在当前Matter，不把本机路径、原始网页或凭证上传。
